We also show how the design principles can be implemented using several examples of Protocol Buffers API design patterns. In particular, we present an intent vocabulary that is based on scopes which allows for decoupling key creation from algorithm identities. It also supports transparent substitutions of algorithms in the applicable scope. Cryptographic governance is enabled by an abstract policy API that does not prescribe the policy format. Keys are represented by stable identifiers and support key evolution operations (rotation, transformation, migration), facilitating migration between algorithms and providers while tracking both the original key identity and its evolution history. With this approach, updating cryptography becomes an operational process without the need to rewrite application code.

</details>


### [51] [Boosting Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12505)
*Pengwei Sun*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: DPOP通过引入参考模型贪婪响应的门控惩罚来增强DPO，在AlpacaEval 2.0上实现了超越多种基线的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线偏好优化方法（如DPO）仅使用数据集中的选择和拒绝响应，忽略了参考模型自身生成的有用信号。

Method: 提出DPOP，在DPO损失基础上增加一个门控惩罚项，仅在当前策略对偏好响应的似然仍低于拒绝响应时激活，惩罚参考模型的贪婪响应。

Result: 在Llama-3-8b-it和Gemma-2-9b-it上，DPOP相比DPO、SimPO和AlphaDPO在长度控制胜率上分别提升5.3%和4.4%。消融实验表明SimNPO风格的长度归一化惩罚优于NPO和token级非似然。

Conclusion: DPOP有效利用了参考模型信号，显著改进了偏好优化效果。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization has become a practical substitute for reinforcement learning from human feedback, but pairwise objectives such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants use only the chosen and rejected responses stored in a static dataset. This leaves a useful signal unused: the response that the reference model itself would generate for the same prompt. We propose Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization (DPOP), a simple extension of DPO that augments the base preference loss with a gated penalty on reference-greedy responses. DPOP activates this penalty only when the current policy still assigns a lower likelihood to the preferred response than to the rejected response. On AlpacaEval 2.0, DPOP improves length-controlled win rate over DPO, SimPO, and AlphaDPO on both Llama-3-8b-it and Gemma-2-9b-it, achieving relative gains of 5.3\% and 4.4\% over baselines on the two models, respectively. Ablations further show that a SimNPO-style length-normalized penalty is stronger than NPO and token-level unlikelihood in this setting.

</details>


### [52] [Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation: Post-Training Without Rubric Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12507)
*MohammadHossein Rezaei,Anas Mahmoud,Zihao Wang,Utkarsh Tyagi,Advait Gosai,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Aakash Sabharwal,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出无验证器的Rubric引导自蒸馏方法(RGSD)，通过条件化教师分布逐token蒸馏替代稀疏轨迹奖励，在医疗和科学领域达到与基于判断器的GRPO相当的评分满意度，且无需训练时调用验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的训练方法依赖LLM验证器逐轮打分，导致训练开销大、引入验证器偏差、反馈稀疏。

Method: 基础策略在条件化评分标准下作为教师，蒸馏至无条件学生模型，实现逐token密集学习信号替代轨迹级稀疏奖励。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5(3B/7B)和Qwen3-Thinking(4B/8B)模型上，RGSD达到与GRPO相当的评分满意度，每提示仅需一次在线策略采样且无验证器调用。

Conclusion: RGSD可作为无验证器替代方案，在验证器成本或可靠性成为瓶颈时互补使用，原始评分标准比自生成参考响应提供更强的教师增强信号。

Abstract: Rubrics have emerged as an alternative to RLVR in open-ended domains where a single ground-truth final answer is not available. Existing rubric-based training methods rely on an LLM verifier that scores each rollout against rubrics. This introduces substantial training-time overhead, exposes optimization to verifier-specific biases, and reduces rubric feedback to a sparse end-of-trajectory signal. We propose Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD), a verifier-free training method in which the base policy, conditioned on the rubric, serves as the teacher for the unconditioned student. RGSD distills the rubric-conditioned teacher distribution into the student token-by-token, replacing sparse trajectory-level rewards with dense per-token learning signals and removing the LLM judge from the training loop entirely. Across Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) and Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) models on medical and science domains, RGSD achieves rubric satisfaction comparable to judge-based GRPO while using one on-policy rollout per prompt and no training-time verifier calls. Ablations show that raw rubrics provide a stronger teacher enrichment signal than self-generated reference responses, while a stronger GRPO judge can outperform RGSD in some settings, positioning RGSD as a complementary verifier-free alternative when verifier cost or reliability is the bottleneck.

</details>


### [53] [Crossing the Validation Crisis: Cross-Validation Reduces Benchmarking Variance Surprisingly Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12552)
*Célestin Eve,Gaël Varoquaux,Thomas Moreau*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文展示交叉验证通过样本增益概念能显著提升机器学习性能评估的置信度，并提出动态早停策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中，由于随机性和有限测试样本，性能评估统计变异性大，导致验证危机，难以辨别真正进展。

Method: 引入样本增益量化多折交叉验证减少基准方差的效果，并设计动态早停程序，通过前几折估计后续样本增益。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集（病理扫描、NLP微调）上，多折交叉验证显著提高性能估计的可靠性和稳定性，且收益递减点晚于预期。

Conclusion: 应充分利用可用样本进行交叉验证，以实现稳健可靠的基准测试。

Abstract: Modern machine learning progresses through empirical work, benchmarking new methods to evaluate relative performance. However, the statistical variability inherent to evaluation - exacerbated by the stochastic nature of many algorithms - often makes performance estimation unreliable due to the limited test samples available, leading to a validation crisis in which genuine advances are difficult to discern. In this work, we show that cross-validation improves markedly confidence when evaluating and comparing learning algorithm performances. We introduce the concept of sample gain, which quantifies the virtual data augmentation achieved by using multiple cross-validation splits to reduce benchmarking variance. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets (histopathologic scans and NLP fine-tuning) demonstrate that multiple splits can substantially improve the reliability and stability of performance estimates, with diminishing returns often setting in later than expected. We also introduce a procedure to dynamically early-stop cross-validation by estimating from the first few folds if subsequent folds will bring large sample gains. Our findings highlight the value of pushing cross-validation on available samples to achieve robust and reliable benchmarking.

</details>


### [54] [Detecting Functional Memorization in Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12764)
*Matthieu Meeus,Anil Ramakrishna,Matthew Grange,Zheng Xu,Luca Melis*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 研究代码生成中大语言模型的功能性记忆现象，发现即使文本不相似，模型也能复现训练数据中的功能逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本重叠指标无法检测功能等价但文本不同的代码记忆，需要更全面的审计方法。

Method: 构建反事实实验，对比Olmo-3-32B中间训练模型（暴露目标代码）与预训练参考模型（未暴露），通过Python函数签名提示，评估文本和功能相似性（LLM评判与执行验证）。

Result: 观察到明确的功能性记忆证据。

Conclusion: 提示超越文本重叠的审计指标对于评估训练数据可恢复性至关重要。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate code at scale. Meanwhile, prior work has investigated whether training data may be recoverable from model outputs, by auditing the textual overlap between training examples and model generations. Code, however, can be functionally equivalent while textually dissimilar. In this work, we study functional memorization: extraction of functional logic beyond what verbatim metrics detect. We construct a counterfactual setup for Olmo-3-32B, comparing a midtrained model (exposed to target code) against a pretrained reference (not exposed). We prompt both models with Python function signatures and measure both textual and functional similarity (i.e., LLM-as-a-judge, execution-based). Our results show clear evidence of functional memorization, highlighting the need for auditing metrics that go beyond textual overlap.

</details>


### [55] [Teach-and-Repeat: Accurately Extracting Operational Knowledge from Mobile Screen Demonstrations to Empower GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12817)
*Yudong Zhang,Lei Hu,Daoyang Liu,Jiawei Liu,Yangfan Luo,Xingyu Liu,Zuojian Wang,Zhilin Gao*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出Teach VLM模型和Teach-and-Repeat范式，从移动屏幕轨迹中提取可重用操作知识，显著提升下游任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM难以准确推断移动设备上高度多样化的UI设计对应的底层操作，且缺乏对齐训练数据。

Method: 通过系统数据飞轮构建训练数据，设计关键帧提取与分析框架，将屏幕轨迹转化为描述操作类型、目标UI元素、文本参数和执行顺序的自然语言句子，并形成可解释的操作知识；后续利用该知识指导下游执行代理。

Result: 在操作语义预测上超越强VLM基线，达到SOTA；在Android World实验中，下游代理任务成功率一致提升。

Conclusion: Teach VLM与Teach-and-Repeat范式提供了一条从原始演示到可重用任务自动化的实用路径。

Abstract: Understanding the digital world on mobile devices is shifting from static UI perception to dynamic action comprehension. This capability enables models to convert visual state transitions into operational knowledge, defined as short natural-language sentences that describe action types, target UI elements, textual arguments, and execution orders. However, due to the highly diverse and heterogeneous UI designs across applications, existing vision-language models (VLMs) struggle to accurately infer these underlying operations. To bridge this gap, we introduce Teach VLM, a core model designed to translate mobile screen trajectories into step-wise operational knowledge by extracting and analyzing operation-related keyframes from demonstration videos. To address the scarcity of aligned training data, we develop a systematic data flywheel for scalable data acquisition. We further introduce a novel Chinese Mobile Screen Teach Benchmark for fine-grained evaluation. Building upon Teach VLM, we propose the Teach-and-Repeat paradigm, where the generated operational knowledge serves as an interpretable procedural reference to guide downstream screen-based execution agents. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Teach VLM significantly outperforms strong VLM baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance in operation semantics prediction. Furthermore, experiments in Android World show that our paradigm yields consistent Task Success Rate improvements for downstream agents. Together, Teach VLM and the Teach-and-Repeat paradigm offer a practical pathway from raw demonstrations to reusable task automation.

</details>


### [56] [The Mathematics of AI Winters: The mathematical Taxonomy of Paradigm Fragility in AI Winter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12610)
*Miquel Noguer i Alonso,David Pacheco Aznar*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出，AI冬天的根本原因不仅是工程与商业失败，还在于当时主导范式遭遇了表征、优化、计算复杂度、统计可学习性及高维逼近等正式数学障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释过于侧重工程与商业层面，缺乏对数学瓶颈的系统分析。

Method: 通过综述感知机不可解结果、神经网络训练NP难、非参数估计最小最大速率、梯度消失及统计学习理论等经典工作，展示数学瓶颈与AI低潮的关联。

Result: 发现早期AI核心挫折与特定数学极限高度一致，而后续突破仅缓解而非消除这些障碍。

Conclusion: 数学瓶颈是AI冬季的重要成因，理解这一历史教训有助于避免未来重蹈覆辙。

Abstract: Two major periods of reduced funding and confidence in artificial intelligence research, commonly called the first and second AI winters, are usually explained through engineering failure, commercial disappointment, and inflated expectations. This article develops a complementary thesis: that the dominant paradigms of those periods also met genuine formal barriers, including limitations of representation, optimisation, computational complexity, statistical learnability, and high-dimensional approximation. The contribution is synthetic rather than archival. We do not claim that particular theorems mechanically caused the winters; rather, we show that several central disappointments of early AI were aligned with mathematically precise bottlenecks. We analyse these bottlenecks through the perceptron impossibility results of Minsky and Papert, the complexity-theoretic hardness of exact neural-network training established by Blum and Rivest, minimax rates for nonparametric estimation in high dimension due to Stone, vanishing-gradient analyses by Hochreiter and by Bengio and collaborators, and classical statistical learning theory in the tradition of Vapnik and Chervonenkis, Valiant, and Blumer and collaborators. We then relate these barriers to the later breakthroughs that mitigated, rather than eliminated, them.

</details>


### [57] [Fantastic Scientific Agents and How to Build Them: AgentBuild for Rietveld Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12834)
*Woong Shin,Craig A. Bridges,Marshall T. McDonnell,Rafael Ferreira da Silva*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: AgentBuild提出了一种将智能体构建作为科学工作流阶段的方法，通过科学家撰写的合同（包含评分标准、课程和知识库）来自动化编译智能体，从而保留科学家的判断力，而非通过微调等方法将其埋没。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体开发方法（如微调、强化学习）会埋没科学家的判断力，而科学工作流正向非确定性智能体转变，需要新的开发范式。

Method: 将智能体构建视为工作流阶段，科学家撰写版本控制的合同（含评分标准、难度分级的课程和外部知识库），由评分驱动法官门控元优化编码智能体，在声明边界内编辑智能体，实现合同编译而非科学家判断编译。

Result: 在X射线衍射数据的Rietveld精修中，AgentBuild通过Li₇La₃Zr₂O₁₂（LLZO）信噪比阶梯的空白构建运行，推进至4小时扫描的前沿案例，揭示了工作流范围限制；评分标准同时奖励可信拟合和轨迹范围，使前沿视为合同失败而非模式拟合失败。

Conclusion: AgentBuild将科学家撰写的合同作为持久资产，基础模型演进时只需重新运行构建（重调而非重建），从而保护科学家的判断力并适应模型变化。

Abstract: As scientific workflows shift from deterministic executables to LLM-based agents, the development practices on offer, such as fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and prompt-and-go, bury the scientist's judgment. We propose treating agent construction as a workflow stage and introduce AgentBuild, which builds a scientific agent from a contract the scientist authors. The contract is a version-controlled rubric, a difficulty-graded curriculum, and a curated external knowledge base. A rubric-driven judge gates a meta-optimizer coding agent that edits the agent within a declared boundary, so the build compiles the agent, not the scientist's judgment. We instantiate this for Rietveld refinement of X-ray diffraction data through GSAS-II behind MCP and A2A, where a blank-harness construction run progresses through a lithium lanthanum zirconium oxide (LLZO) signal-to-noise ladder, reaches the 4 hour scan as a frontier case, and exposes the workflow-scope limits that remain. The same rubric that rewards credible fits also scores trajectory scope, making the frontier a contract failure rather than a pattern-fitting failure. As base models evolve, re-running AgentBuild is a re-tune, not a rebuild, and the scientist's authored contract remains the durable asset.

</details>


### [58] [Bag of Dims: Training-Free Mechanistic Interpretability via Dimension-Level Sign Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12629)
*Varun Reddy Nalagatla*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 标准基维度独立编码语义（符号）和置信度（幅值），无需训练即可作为特征基，跨模型验证了维度间极低耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer隐藏状态的标准基是否已具备可解释的特征基能力，避免依赖训练、优化或大量计算。

Method: 通过四种渐进实验：符号预测、单类型缓存聚类、注意力投影分析、FFN权重回溯验证维度编码，并采用无监督随机种子扩展特征。

Result: 仅符号模式实现72-93% top-5准确率；无训练聚类发现175个语义类别（平均AUC 0.80）；训练探针仅+0.018 AUC且收敛到轴对齐权重；FFN中20%特征对应单写神经元（一致性>0.70）；无监督扩展到1500特征100%且99%稀疏。

Conclusion: 标准基已足够在Transformer全计算路径中读取特征，无需训练或优化，仅需每个词汇标记前向一次。

Abstract: We show that the standard basis of transformer hidden states already provides a training-free, architecture-general feature basis. Individual dimensions encode semantic content via their signs and confidence via their magnitudes, functioning as independent binary registers. We validate this Bag of Dims framework across three model families (Qwen 3.5-4B, Gemma 3-4B, Mistral 7B) through four progressive experiments. Sign patterns alone carry predictive content: replacing all magnitudes with unity achieves 72-93% top-5 next-token accuracy through the LM head, and pure Hamming scoring without any decoder reaches 80-90% top-4096. These sign patterns organize into semantic features: using a single-token type cache (one forward pass per vocabulary token, no context), we discover 175 categories via per-dimension sign consistency (mean AUC 0.80) from 50 anchors with zero training. A trained probe adds only +0.018 AUC and converges to axis-aligned weights, confirming negligible cross-dimension structure. This structure extends to attention: all 175 categories remain discoverable in K and V projections. On the write side, static FFN weight inspection links 20% of features to individual writer neurons (>0.70 agreement; random controls: 0%), with top-200 neuron coalitions achieving >0.70 agreement on 99.9% of prototypes via majority vote. Fully unsupervised discovery (random seeds, no labels) scales to 1500 features at 100% yield and 99% sparsity across all three models, with pairwise MI of 0.0014 bits confirming low inter-dimension coupling. These results establish that the standard basis already suffices for feature reading throughout the transformer compute pathway, requiring no training, no optimization, and no GPU-days beyond a single forward pass per vocabulary token.

</details>


### [59] [The Metric Picks the Winner: Evaluation Choice Flips Model Rankings for Drug-Response Prediction in Unseen Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12639)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Riya Bisht*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 预测药物转录组响应的基准测试表明，评价指标的选择会完全逆转模型排名，深度模型在真实指标上显著优于简单基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准显示复杂模型在药物化学结构外推时难以超越简单基线，需要研究指标选择对模型评估的影响。

Method: 提出三阶段方法：简单基线、基于Tanimoto加权的非参数检索、融合化学嵌入与检索特征的残差预测解码器，并加入不确定性头和基因程序。

Result: 在Bemis-Murcko骨架划分下，基于方差逆权重代理指标时线性回归获胜；但在VCPI竞赛真实活性加权指标下，深度融合解码器以-0.012 wMSE显著优于线性基线（p<10⁻⁴），代理指标的胜者变为最差模型。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Predicting how a cell's transcriptome responds to a drug it has never seen is a core, hard problem in computational cell biology: recent benchmarks show complex models often fail to beat trivial baselines once test compounds are held out by chemistry. We study one cell line and assay, THP-1 cells profiled by DRUG-seq, scored by the active-compound weighted MSE(wMSE) of the VCPI prediction contest. We propose a staged approach: dumb baselines (untreated control and mean training-compound response) that the field keeps failing to beat; non-parametric retrieval (a Tanimoto-weighted average of a held-out compound's nearest training compounds); and a fusion stage combining a frozen chemistry embedding with retrieval-support features to predict the residual over the mean, with an uncertainty head and gene programs. On the released VCPI THP-1 drug-seq data (14,026 training compounds), under a Bemis-Murcko scaffold split, the model ranking inverts depending on the metric. Under an inverse-variance per-gene proxy, a regularized linear regression on Morgan fingerprints appears to win over the deep models, retrieval, and ChemBERTa -- the textbook "simple baselines win" result. But under the contest's true active-set metric (per-(gene, compound) Mejia weights, validated against the official scorer; mean baseline 0.535 vs the organizers' 0.507 reference), that reverses: the deep models win, our fusion decoder significantly beats the linear fingerprint baseline (-0.012 wMSE, paired bootstrap p < 10^-4), and the proxy's winner becomes the worst chemistry-aware predictor. Picking the metric picks the winner -- to our knowledge the first demonstration on real held-out drug chemistry of the metric-calibration effect established largely on genetic perturbation. We release a reproducible pipeline
  wired to the official scorer that emits a valid submission over the real 1064 x 12,995 grid.

</details>


### [60] [DailyReport: An Open-ended Benchmark for Evaluating Search Agents on Daily Search Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12871)
*Jingxuan Han,Wei Liu,Mingyang Zhu,Youpeng Wang,Ziwen Wang,Lin Qiu,Xuezhi Cao,Xunliang Cai,Zheren Fu,Licheng Zhang,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出了DailyReport基准，包含150个开放任务和3546个级联评分标准，用于评估搜索代理在真实日常搜索场景中的性能，发现现有系统仍达不到用户期望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理评估基准专注于专业任务且使用粗粒度评分标准，缺乏对真实用户场景的覆盖和可解释性。

Method: 构建DailyReport基准，将任务分解为子任务并设计级联评分标准，通过级联性能归因和用户中心聚合得到各维度可解释分数及用户偏好分数。

Result: 在17个智能体系统上的评估结果表明当前系统未达到用户期望。

Conclusion: DailyReport为搜索代理在真实日常任务上的评估提供了更全面的基准，有助于推动未来发展。

Abstract: Search Agents (SAs) typically leverage large language models (LLMs) to support complex information-seeking tasks by autonomously exploring web sources and synthesizing information into comprehensive responses. For SAs evaluation, prior benchmarks mainly focus on specialized tasks that are unlikely to arise in real-world user scenarios. Moreover, their reliance on coarse task-level rubrics often limits evaluation interpretability. To bridge this gap, we introduce DailyReport, an open-ended benchmark to evaluate SA capabilities on daily search tasks. It contains 150 open-ended tasks with 3,546 associated rubrics, capturing widely discussed and timely information demands of real-world users. Each task is decomposed into subtasks and evaluated with cascade rubrics across disentangled dimensions. Through cascade performance attribution and user-centric aggregation, we derive highly interpretable scores for each dimension, along with a user preference score. Our results on 17 agentic systems show that current systems still fall short of users' expectations. To facilitate future research, our dataset and code are made publicly available at https://github.com/AGI-Eval-Official/DailyReport.

</details>


### [61] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Chemotherapy Pharmacokinetics: Benchmarking the Clinical Estimator and Exposing Parameter Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12658)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: PINN在化疗药代动力学中匹配NLS在可解问题上的性能，同时在无闭式解问题上揭示并缓解不可辨识性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物部分可观测问题中，已知动力学但某些隔室不可测，传统方法（NLS）在非线性模型中因闭式解缺失而失效，且无法整合稀疏额外观测。

Method: 基于物理信息神经网络（PINN），将已知ODE约束加入损失函数，同时拟合血浆数据和（可选）组织样本，无需闭式解。

Result: 线性模型：PINN匹配NLS（误差小常数倍），同时输出组织曲线；Michaelis-Menten模型：NLS返回无意义参数，PINN暴露不可辨识性（k12→0），加入两个组织观测后恢复除k12外的参数。

Conclusion: PINN提供统一框架，在经典问题中达到经典方法性能，在复杂问题中揭示结构可辨识性，并能灵活融合异质数据。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are an attractive tool for partial-observation problems in biology, where the governing dynamics are known but some compartments cannot be measured. Chemotherapy pharmacokinetics (PK) is a clean instance: drug concentration in plasma is routinely measured, but concentration in tissue -- which determines tumour kill and off-target toxicity -- is not. We benchmark a PINN against the standard clinical baseline (nonlinear least-squares on the analytical biexponential plasma solution, hereafter NLS) and a physics-agnostic neural baseline (a data-only MLP) on two PK problems. On the linear two-compartment problem, NLS is near-optimal; the PINN matches it to within a small constant factor while also producing the tissue curve in a single training pass, whereas the data-only MLP fails on tissue by roughly 10x. On a Michaelis-Menten extension (saturable elimination), the biexponential closed form no longer exists, so NLS is mis-specified and silently returns meaningless rate constants. The PINN instead exposes a deeper fact: the Michaelis-Menten two-compartment model is non-identifiable from plasma alone, and the PINN reports this honestly by converging to a basin with k12 -> 0. Adding two sparse tissue observations largely resolves identifiability: across five seeds the PINN recovers k21 to within 1% of truth and Vmax, Km to within one standard-deviation bar, while k12 moves in the correct direction (0.02 -> 0.82) but remains ~2 sigma below truth -- a recovery the closed-form NLS estimator cannot attempt at all, because its biexponential ansatz describes only plasma. Our claim is not that PINNs beat NLS. It is that PINNs offer a uniform recipe that ties the textbook estimator on the textbook problem, exposes structural identifiability that
  the textbook estimator hides, and absorbs heterogeneous measurements within a single loss.

</details>


### [62] [OpenMedQ: Broad Open Pretraining for Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12953)
*Ibrahim Gulluk,Max Van Puyvelde,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: OpenMedQ是一个基于大规模开源医学数据预训练的视觉语言模型，在PathVQA和VQA-MED等任务上达到最先进性能，其视觉编码器在8个医学分类基准上取得最高平均macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型多依赖封闭数据或小规模开源数据，缺乏可复现的强基线。OpenMedQ旨在通过整合最大规模的完全开源医学混合数据集（14个数据集，约335万样本），建立一个可复现且性能领先的基线模型。

Method: 使用涵盖病理学、放射学、显微镜和纯文本临床问答的14个数据集共约335万样本进行预训练，构建视觉语言模型OpenMedQ；并对视觉编码器进行迁移学习，在8个未见过的医学分类任务上评估。

Result: 在PathVQA上BLEU-1达到75.9，超越高达562B参数的Med-PaLM M变体；在VQA-MED上BLEU-1为64.5，与最佳报告结果持平；视觉编码器在8个分类基准上平均macro-F1为0.757，高于BiomedCLIP（0.745）、PMC-CLIP（0.745）、PubMedCLIP（0.746）和从头训练基线（0.616）。

Conclusion: OpenMedQ通过大规模开源数据预训练，在多个医学视觉语言任务和分类任务上达到最先进性能，代码和交互演示已公开，可作为社区的可复现基线。

Abstract: We present OpenMedQ, a medical vision-language model pretrained on the broadest fully-open medical mix to date: 14 datasets totaling ~3.35M pretraining samples spanning pathology, radiology, microscopy, and text-only clinical QA. OpenMedQ reaches state-of-the-art BLEU-1 on PathVQA (75.9), beating Med-PaLM M variants up to 562B parameters (~80x larger), and matches the best reported VQA-MED BLEU-1 (64.5). Its vision encoder, transferred to 8 unseen medical classification benchmarks under an identical downstream recipe, obtains the highest average macro-F1 (0.757) among BiomedCLIP (0.745), PMC-CLIP (0.745), PubMedCLIP (0.746), and a from-scratch baseline (0.616). We release our code and an interactive demo is publicly available as a reproducible baseline for the community.

</details>


### [63] [Two-Layer Linear Auto-Regressive Models Estimate Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12691)
*Yahya Sattar,Sunmook Choi,Leo Maynard-Zhang,Yassir Jedra,Maryam Fazel,Sarah Dean*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文证明，在部分观测线性动力系统上训练的线性自回归模型可以自然地逼近卡尔曼滤波，其隐藏状态表示与最优状态估计等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自回归模型在序列数据中学习隐层表示的内在机制，特别是其与经典最优滤波理论（如卡尔曼滤波）的联系。

Method: 通过理论分析，证明线性自回归模型的训练等价于逼近卡尔曼滤波，并给出截断误差、非凸优化景观（鞍点或全局最小值）以及有限样本保证。

Result: 自回归模型能够恢复状态估计，且预测误差、参数估计误差和隐状态恢复均具有有限样本保证。

Conclusion: 线性自回归模型在特定条件下内在地学习最优滤波策略，无需显式建模动力系统即可近似卡尔曼滤波。

Abstract: Auto-regressive models have emerged as powerful tools for sequential data, from language to video. Understanding how and why these models learn latent representations remains an open theoretical question. In this work, we demonstrate that when trained by empirical risk minimization on data from partially observed linear dynamical systems, two-layer linear auto-regressive models naturally learn to approximate Kalman filtering. In particular, we show that the learned hidden representation coincides, up to a similarity transformation, with the state estimates produced by the optimal (Kalman) filter, even though the model has no explicit knowledge of the underlying dynamics or state. The result follows from three main insights. First, we establish that the Kalman filter is well approximated by an auto-regressive model with bounded truncation error. Second, we show that despite non-convexity, the two-layer optimization landscape is benign, i.e., all stationary points are either strict saddles or global minima. Finally, as our main contributions, we provide finite-sample guarantees on prediction error, parameter estimation error, and latent state recovery. Numerical simulations support the theoretical results and demonstrate that the latent representations of auto-regressive models recover state estimates.

</details>


### [64] [Let's Ask Gauss: Improved One-Run Privacy Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12733)
*Adya Agrawal,Yu Wei,Jaspal Singh,Malik Magdon-Ismail,Vassilis Zikas*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于高斯渐近分布的白盒DP-SGD隐私审计框架，通过规范化和累积候选信号实现单次训练更紧的隐私下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次审计方法对训练样本或候选信号进行二值成员猜测，丢弃了有用信息，导致隐私下界不紧。

Method: 证明白盒DP-SGD中候选对齐信号天然形成随机变量序列，其归一化和渐近服从高斯分布，并基于此分布特性设计审计框架。

Result: 单次训练可得到更紧的隐私下界，提升审计效率。

Conclusion: 利用信号的分布特性可有效改进差分隐私机器学习中的实证审计紧致性。

Abstract: Privacy auditing provides an important safeguard by estimating the actual information leaked by a model, thus ensuring that theoretical privacy guarantees hold in practice. We study empirical privacy auditing for differentially private (DP) machine learning, focusing on efficient one-run methods for mechanisms such as DP-SGD. Prior one-run approaches threshold training examples or "canaries" into binary membership guesses, which discards useful information. We show that, in the white-box DP-SGD setting, canary-aligned signals naturally form a sequence of random variables whose normalized sum is asymptotically Gaussian. Leveraging this distributional perspective, we develop a DP-auditing framework that leads to tighter privacy lower bounds from a single training run.

</details>


### [65] [Deep Unfolded Latent Optimally Partitioned-l2/l1 Networks for Data-driven Block-Sparse Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12740)
*Takanobu Furuhashi,Hidekata Hontani,Qibin Zhao,Tatsuya Yokota*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于深度展开的LOP-l2/l1架构，解决了原方法需要手动调参和数值不稳定的问题，在块稀疏信号恢复中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸LOP-l2/l1方法依赖手动超参数调参，且其近端算子微分存在数值不稳定，无法通过深度展开实现自动参数学习。

Method: 提出了两种架构：基于隐式微分的稳定框架和基于深度权重分解的灵活变体，后者还支持非凸光滑数据保真项。

Result: 数值实验表明，深度展开的LOP-l2/l1方法性能优越，且对脉冲噪声具有高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了原方法的调参困难和数值不稳定问题，提升了块稀疏信号恢复的性能和噪声鲁棒性。

Abstract: The convex Latent Optimal Partition (LOP)-l2/l1 approach enables block-sparse signal recovery with unknown partitions but relies on manual hyperparameter tuning. Additionally, numerical instability in differentiating its proximal operator prevents its automatic parameter tuning via Deep Unfolding (DU). To address these limitations, we propose two architectures: a stable framework utilizing implicit differentiation and a flexible variant leveraging Deep Weight Factorization (DWF). The DWF-based approach also supports nonconvex smooth data fidelity terms. Numerical experiments demonstrate that DU-LOP-l2/l1 yields competitive performance and high resilience against impulsive noise.

</details>


### [66] [AAbAAC: An Annotated Corpus for Autoimmunity Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13051)
*Fabien Maury,Solène Grosdidier,Maud de Dieuleveult,Adrien Coulet*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文构建了自身免疫领域标注语料库AAbAAC，并通过微调提升了命名实体识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大模型在高度专业化的生物医学领域（如自身免疫）存在性能差距。

Method: 从PubMed选取115篇摘要，手动标注实体及其关系，构建AAbAAC语料库，用于评估和微调NER模型。

Result: 微调后NER性能显著提升，验证了小规模标注在专业领域的有效性。

Conclusion: AAbAAC语料库推动了自身免疫领域的信息抽取研究。

Abstract: Despite advances in information extraction driven by deep learning and large language models, performance gaps remain in highly specialized biomedical fields, where domainspecific complexity poses challenges for generalist models. In this work, we focus on the domain of autoimmunity, where the main entities of interest are autoimmune diseases, autoantibodies (i.e., molecules that may mark or cause these diseases), their molecular targets, their location in the body, and their associated clinical signs. Herein, we present AAbAAC (AutoAntibodies and Autoimmunity Annotated Corpus), a corpus of 115 abstracts selected from PubMed, where we manually annotated entities and their relationships. First, AAbAAC was used to evaluate several methods on the task of named entity recognition (NER), and secondly, to fine-tune NER models. Our study demonstrates the utility of AAbAAC for information extraction in the domain of autoimmunity, showing expected improvement in NER performance after finetuning. This illustrates the value of small-scale annotation efforts for specialized domains and contributes to the computational study of autoimmunity. The AAbAAC corpus is available at https://github.com/f-maury/AAbAAC.

</details>


### [67] [EPIG: Emotion-Based Prompting for Personalised Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13247)
*Emna Othmen,Mohamed Yassine Landolsi,Lotfi Ben Romdhane*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出EPIG方法，通过在提示层面增强情感表达，利用心理学效价-唤醒模型和角色感知的结构化提示增强，在不修改或重新训练模型的情况下，提升扩散模型生成图像的情感一致性，尤其在控制唤醒度方面有效，且轻量级、无需训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本到图像扩散模型的提示策略较为通用，难以准确表达情感意图和细微情感属性，限制了生成图像的情感表达力。

Method: 基于心理学效价-唤醒的情感表示，结合结构化、角色感知的提示增强，在图像生成前丰富提示中的情感相关成分，不修改或重新训练生成模型。

Result: 在10个多样化提示的基准上，与朴素插入和基于LLM的提示扩展相比，平均唤醒误差分别降低14%和12%，差异显著；保留效价对齐和语义一致性（通过CLIPScore测量）；在包含显式主体（如人、儿童、动物）的提示上效果更明显，误差降低17%。

Conclusion: EPIG是一种轻量、无需训练的情感增强方法，能有效提升图像生成的情感表达能力，尤其适用于资源受限和个性化生成场景，且对主体敏感的提示有更强效果。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models have achieved impressive results in synthesizing high-quality images from natural language prompts. However, commonly used prompting strategies remain relatively generic, limiting the model's ability to accurately express emotional intent and nuanced affective attributes. This work proposes EPIG, a method that enhances emotional expressiveness at the prompt level prior to image generation. Grounded in psychologically informed emotion representations (valence-arousal) and leveraging structured, role-aware prompt enrichment, EPIG enriches emotion-related components of prompts without modifying or retraining the image generation backbone. The resulting emotion-aware prompts guide the generative process toward more emotionally coherent visual outputs, with particular effectiveness in controlling arousal. EPIG is lightweight, training-free, and well suited for resource-constrained and personalized image generation scenarios. Experimental results on a benchmark of 10 diverse prompts show that EPIG reduces mean arousal error compared to strong baselines, including naive insertion and LLM-based prompt expansion, with reductions of 14% and 12%, respectively. These improvements are statistically significant. EPIG also preserves valence alignment and semantic consistency, as measured by CLIPScore and supported by ablation studies. The effect is more pronounced on prompts containing explicit subjects such as humans, children, or animals, where the reduction reaches 17%, highlighting the subject-sensitive behavior of the proposed method.

</details>


### [68] [Order Is Not Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12923)
*Gareth Seneque,Lap-Hang Ho,Nafise Erfanian Saeedi,Jeffrey Molendijk,Tim Elson*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文论证‘序≠控制’，提出接收器门控响应定律，在生物、大模型、适配器与随机算子面板中验证，区分局部可允许控制与不可控范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正将‘有序结构’等同于‘可控’的倾向，建立以接收器为中心的响应定律作为控制判据，避免坐标同一性或控制器假设的误判。

Method: 定义分母索引算子的接收器门控响应律，在ALM小鼠、线虫、斑马鱼、LLM输出（预测成分符号精度72.8–84.8%）、适配器与随机算子面板中，通过驱动-耗散框架测量运动、阻抗、沉没与过驱。

Result: LLM输出响应向量符号预测精度72.8–73.7%（全分量），84.3–84.8%（非零分量）；观察者预测系统效应93.6%、目标/原型族91.7%。明确定位了局部可允许控制、随机响应算子，排除了坐标同一性、隐藏因果充分性与字面热力学量。

Conclusion: 控制是局部的、接收器门控的、驱动-耗散的，而非由有序结构或坐标同一性赋予。

Abstract: AI alignment, interpretability, steering, and neural perturbation studies identify order-inducing objects. We argue that order is not control. Control requires a receiver-gated response law: a denominator-indexed operator mapping material state, action/drive, bath, and receiver state to response displacement, sinks, effort, and basin projection. We identify it across biological, LLM, adapter, and stochastic-operator panels. The laws are local: an intervention can be admitted, saturated, sign-changing, leaky, or overdriven depending on medium, bath, receiver state, action port, and comparator. Control is assigned when finite effort moves a target or outcome-readout class under the same denominator while damage, null/evasive, invalid format, overdrive, and unnecessary effort stay bounded. Mouse ALM, C. elegans, and zebrafish panels provide physical response-operator evidence while excluding coordinate identity and controller conclusions. LLM panels show generated-output response laws: across four material conditions, response vectors are predictable at 72.8-73.7% component-sign accuracy, rising to 84.3-84.8% on nonzero components; held-out observers predict system-effect and target/oracle families at 93.6% and 91.7% accuracy. Constitution-conditioned adapters reshape susceptibility as prepared media, and stochastic-operator panels separate measured opportunity from deployable action policies. This gives a driven-dissipative response-system account at the mesoscopic control level: drives act through prepared media, baths, and receivers, producing admitted movement, impedance, sinks, or overdrive. The evidence supports local admitted control and measurable stochastic response operators, while leaving deployable pre-generation control, hidden/logit causal sufficiency, biological-to-LLM coordinate identity, and literal thermodynamic quantities outside scope.

</details>


### [69] [Circuit Synchronization Precedes Generalization: Causal Evidence from Fourier Structure in Grokking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12966)
*Achyuthan Sivasankar*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出频率同步度（FSD）指标，可在Transformer出现模算术突现（grokking）前500-3000步检测到电路同步，并发现突现延迟与权重衰减成反比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽将突现归因于傅里叶电路，但对其时序、因果结构和可控性理解不足，缺乏无需先验电路知识的早期预测指标。

Method: 定义归一化且经置换检验的FSD度量，通过分叉训练控制权重衰减，在多种模加法配置上进行验证并进行架构消融实验。

Result: FSD在9种配置中均领先突现约1722步，是最早的预测指标；突现时间差与1/λ严格单调相关（R²=1.00/0.99）；仅注意力模型能突现并具有FSD前兆，仅MLP模型不能突现。

Conclusion: FSD是突现的早期因果前兆，突现延迟是正则化现象，由权重衰减控制，且该电路特性需要多层注意力结构。

Abstract: Grokking -- where a transformer on modular arithmetic suddenly transitions from near-chance to near-perfect validation accuracy -- is attributed to a Fourier circuit, but its timing, causal structure, and controllability remain poorly understood. We introduce the Frequency Synchronization Degree (FSD), a normalised, permutation-tested metric for Fourier circuit synchronisation requiring no prior circuit knowledge. Across nine modular addition configurations (primes p in {53, 71, 97, 113, 131}, three seeds), FSD synchronises 500-3,000 steps before grokking (mean lead +1,722 steps; all nine positive, sign-test p~0.004), and precedes a restricted-logit loss baseline (Nanda et al.'s excluded loss) in all nine cases, making it the earliest available predictor. We provide direct causal evidence that the inter-phase gap is a regularisation phenomenon: forking training at the FSD-ceiling step and varying weight decay lambda produces strictly monotone earlier grokking, with Delta_t proportional to 1/lambda. This law replicates across three primes (p in {53,97,131}; R^2=1.00 and R^2=0.99 for two clean cases), captured as Delta_t ~ C/lambda, consistent with (1/lambda)*log(||W_mem||/tau). Architecture ablations show an attention-only model groks with a strong FSD precursor; an MLP-only model never groks; a single-layer model's FSD lags, confirming the precursor is a multi-block circuit property.

</details>


### [70] [Physics-Guided Spatiotemporal Learning for Coastal Wave Peak Period Estimation from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13302)
*Abubakar Hamisu Kamagata,Dharm Singh Jat,Attlee Munyaradzi Gamundani,Abhishek Srivastava,Paramasivam Saravanakumar*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理引导的深度学习框架，从近岸视频中估计波峰周期，结合迁移学习与物理正则化，提高了预测精度与物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波参数监测系统成本高、空间覆盖有限，现有深度学习方法缺乏物理可解释性与海洋学验证。

Method: 构建物理引导的深度时空学习框架，包括基于时间方差的兴趣区检测、多阶段仿真到真实迁移学习、物理信息正则化，并评估了多种时空架构。

Result: Transformer架构瞬时预测精度高，轻量级循环卷积架构时间稳定性与海洋学技能更优；物理正则化改善了趋势一致性并减少了不合理预测。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了物理引导的视频深度学习系统在低成本、可操作的长周期近岸波监测中的潜力。

Abstract: Wave parameters in the nearshore are crucial for coastal engineering, shoreline protection, marine hazard assessment, and coastal management for climate resilience. Traditional monitoring systems like buoys and radar platforms offer accurate monitoring but can have high installation and maintenance expenses and limited spatial coverage. Passive ocean monitoring using video has been achieved by leveraging deep learning, however, many methods are not physically interpretable, feasible, and validated for oceanography. In thiswork, a Physics-Guided Deep Spatiotemporal Learning Framework for direct estimation of nearshore wave peak periods from passive coastal video stream is proposed. The framework combines automated temporal-variance based region-of-interest detection, multi-stage Sim-to-Real transfer learning, and physics-informed regularization to enhance the predictive accuracy and physical consistency. A variety of spatiotemporal architectures were assessed, such as transformer-based and recurrent-convolutional ones, alongside synthetic pretraining,silver-label adaptation, and expert fine-tuning. The results show that transformer-based architectures outperformed in terms of the accuracy of the instantaneous prediction, while lightweight recurrent-convolutional architectures achieved higher temporal stability and operational oceanographic skill. Ablation studies also demonstrated the benefits of physics-guided regularization in terms of trend-following consistency, and physically implausible predictions. Explainability auditing also helped to focus attention in hydrodynamically active surf-zone regions and showed good agreement with the physically derived wave propagation behavior. In general, the proposed framework shows the promise of physics-guided video-based deep learning systems for long-term coastal wave monitoring that are cost-efficient and operationally feasible.

</details>


### [71] [IterCAD: An Iterative Multimodal Agent for Visually-Grounded CAD Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13368)
*Tao Hu,Jiaxin Ai,Licheng Wen,Xueheng Li,Shu Zou,Siqi Li,Nianchen Deng,Xinyu Cai,Hongbin Zhou,Pinlong Cai,Daocheng Fu,Yu Yang,Hairong Zhang,Botian Shi,Xuemeng Yang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出了IterCAD，一个统一的多模态智能体框架，用于闭环、交互式的计算机辅助设计（CAD）生成与编辑，通过渐进式监督微调和几何感知强化学习优化，在代码可执行性和几何精度上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化CAD方法主要依赖开环、一次性生成，与迭代式的真实实践不匹配，缺乏交互性和灵活性。

Method: 将任务建模为多模态智能体与可执行CAD沙盒的多轮交互，涵盖绘图到代码、文本到代码和交互编辑三种任务；开发了数据合成流程以生成符合工业标准的工程图纸和交互轨迹；采用渐进式监督微调后结合几何感知强化学习（含可行前缀掩码）优化智能体。

Result: 通过提出的IterCAD-Bench评估套件和Chamfer Distance Tolerance-Recall曲线及AUC-TR指标，实验表明IterCAD在多个基准上取得竞争性表现，在代码可执行性和几何精度上显著优于现有方法，并展现出优越的闭环迭代精化能力。

Conclusion: IterCAD通过闭环交互框架和优化策略，有效弥合了现有自动化CAD与真实实践之间的差距，为交互式CAD生成和编辑提供了更可靠和精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Computer-Aided Design is pivotal in modern manufacturing, yet existing automated methods predominantly rely on open-loop, one-shot generation, creating a mismatch with iterative real-world practices. In this paper, we present IterCAD, a unified multimodal agent framework for closed-loop, interactive CAD generation and editing. We formulate the task as a multi-turn interaction between a multimodal agent and an executable CAD sandbox, covering three tasks: Drawing-to-Code, Text-to-Code, and Interactive Editing. To support this, we develop a data synthesis pipeline incorporating advanced industrial manufacturing features to generate standard-compliant multi-view engineering drawings, complex code-editing tasks, and high-fidelity interaction trajectories. We optimize the agent via progressive SFT followed by geometry-aware reinforcement learning with viable-prefix masking to enhance code executability and geometric fidelity. Finally, we introduce the IterCAD-Bench evaluation suite and propose the Chamfer Distance Tolerance-Recall (CD-TR) curve alongside its AUC-TR metric, establishing a survivor-bias-free standard that unifies code validity and geometric precision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IterCAD achieves highly competitive performance across multiple benchmarks, significantly outperforming existing approaches in both code executability and geometric precision, while exhibiting superior capabilities in closed-loop iterative refinement.

</details>


### [72] [A Quantitative Experimental Repeated Measures Study of Training Dynamics in a Small Llama Style Language Model Under a Compute-Aware Token Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13370)
*Joe Dwyer*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 在固定计算预算下，小规模语言模型的训练动态表明，额外训练可能带来性能退化，评估应关注中间轨迹而非终点指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估仅依赖终点性能，可能掩盖训练过程中的不稳定性、回退和收益递减现象，尤其在计算受限场景下。

Method: 采用定量实验重复测量设计，在4.26百万参数Llama式模型上使用TinyStories语料进行6次独立训练，采集21个区间的验证损失、困惑度、波动性等指标，使用重复测量方差分析。

Result: 验证损失从初始8.3552降至约4百万token时的2.7996，但最终升至3.9010；困惑度呈先降后升趋势；存在频繁的验证损失回退现象，无稳定阶段。

Conclusion: 计算感知的语言模型评估应分析训练轨迹而非仅终点指标；在受限计算下，增加token可能增加成本而不带来等比例泛化收益。

Abstract: This study examines training dynamics in a small Llama-style language model trained under a fixed, compute-constrained token budget. Rather than evaluating efficiency solely through endpoint performance, the study uses a quantitative experimental repeated measures design to analyze how validation loss, validation perplexity, rolling volatility, backslide behavior, spike behavior, and between-seed variability change across token-based training intervals. Six independent training runs were conducted on a 4.26-million-parameter model using the TinyStories corpus, CPU-based full-precision training, and a target budget of approximately 20 million cumulative training tokens. Metrics were collected across 21 intervals, producing 126 seed-by-interval observations. Repeated measures ANOVA showed statistically significant interval effects for validation loss, validation perplexity, and rolling volatility. Descriptive trajectories revealed rapid early improvement followed by non-monotonic degradation during later training intervals. Mean validation loss decreased from 8.3552 at initialization to 2.7996 near 4 million tokens, but increased to 3.9010 by the final checkpoint. Validation perplexity followed the same pattern, falling sharply early in training before rising later. Derived telemetry further showed recurrent validation-loss backslides and no interval-summary evidence of a stable phase under the predefined criteria. These findings suggest that compute-aware language model evaluation should examine training trajectories rather than endpoint metrics alone. In constrained compute settings, additional token exposure may increase computational cost without producing proportional generalization gains, and interval-level telemetry can reveal instability, regression, and diminishing returns that final metrics may obscure.

</details>


### [73] [Optimizing Appliance Scheduling for Solar Energy Management Using Metaheuristic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13407)
*Hiba Ahmed,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Jason Adair,Simon T. Powers*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于迭代局部搜索和模拟退火的元启发式方法，用于优化家用电器启动时间，以最大化太阳能利用率并最小化用户不便，支持跨多天的连续性调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳能发电仅在白天进行，与用户偏好的家电使用时间不匹配，现有研究多局限于单日调度，未考虑未完成任务跨日延续的问题。

Method: 采用迭代局部搜索和模拟退火算法，结合家电运行时长、功耗、逆变器限制、电池状态及太阳辐射预测，构建多日连续调度框架。

Result: 实验表明该多日顺序调度框架能在保证用户便利性前提下有效管理系统约束，并实现纯太阳能供电。

Conclusion: 该方法为未来研究多目标权衡（如设备投资、回报与用户满意度）提供了基础。

Abstract: Renewable energy is essential for meeting future energy demands; however, solar energy generation, which occurs only during daylight hours often does not align with household consumption patterns. Appliances such as cookers, washing machines, and dryers are typically operated according to user preferred schedules rather than solar energy availability, creating a scheduling optimization problem. The objective is to determine optimal appliance start times to maximize renewable energy utilization while minimizing user inconvenience and adhering to system constraints. This paper presents a metaheuristic approach using Iterated Local Search (ILS) and Simulated Annealing (SA) to optimize appliance start times, while considering appliance operating durations, power consumption, inverter limit, battery state of charge constraints, and solar generation forecasts. Unlike most existing work, the scheduling is extended beyond a single day to accommodate unfinished tasks from previous days (spillover), ensuring operational continuity and enabling sequential operation across multiple days. Experimental results show that the sequential multi-day scheduling framework effectively manages system constraints while ensuring user convenience under exclusive solar generation. These findings also open opportunities for future research on multi-objective trade-offs between investment in equipment of various sizes, return on that investment, and user satisfaction.

</details>


### [74] [Evaluation Sovereignty in Metadata-Driven Classification: A Multi-Track Framework for Weakly Supervised Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13436)
*Raymond Vasquez*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 评估主权取决于标签权威和监管体制，多轨道评估框架揭示操作标签下的高性能在独立评估中显著下降，表明常见指标可能反映与标签过程的对齐而非真实预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习评估通常被视为中性，但在操作信息系统中评估结果受标签生成过程影响，现有方法忽视标签权威对性能测量的有效性影响。

Method: 引入评估主权概念，提出多轨道评估框架，系统化改变训练和评估标签来源，并在分层多标签分类的大规模科学元数据上实施。

Result: 在操作（‘银标’）评估下性能强的模型在独立（‘金标’）评估下大幅下降（Micro-F1从0.54降至0.03），但排名类指标仍高于基线，显示潜在模型信号与分类有效性之间的分歧。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Evaluation in machine learning is typically treated as a neutral measurement process. However, in operational information systems, evaluation outcomes are often conditioned by the processes used to generate labels. This paper does not seek to improve classification performance. Instead, it examines the validity of performance measurement under differing label-authority regimes. This issue is particularly relevant in large-scale metadata-driven systems, where labels are often incomplete, inconsistent, or weakly supervised.
  We introduce evaluation sovereignty, defined as the degree to which performance metrics are independent of label authority and supervision regime, and propose a multi-track evaluation framework that systematically varies training and evaluation label sources. Using hierarchical multi-label classification on large-scale scientific metadata, we demonstrate that models exhibiting strong performance under operational ("silver") evaluation degrade substantially under independent ("gold") evaluation, particularly for fine-grained classification. For example, Micro-F1 decreases from approximately 0.54 to 0.03. Notably, ranking-based metrics remain above baseline, revealing a divergence between latent model signal and classification validity.
  These findings suggest that commonly reported performance metrics may reflect alignment with labeling processes rather than true predictive capability. We therefore reconceptualize evaluation validity as a system-level property shaped by label governance and provide a practical methodology for auditing intelligent systems operating under weak supervision.

</details>


### [75] [Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Long-Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13550)
*Hoin Jung,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的混合粒度检索框架UMG-RAG，通过不确定性估计融合多粒度检索证据，并引入父块提升策略以优化生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中检索单元粒度选择存在矛盾：大单元保留上下文但引入噪音，小单元紧凑但检索可靠性低，现有方法无法动态适配查询需求。

Method: 利用现有稠密和稀疏检索器作为多粒度互补专家，将每个专家的得分列表转换为证据分布，通过分布熵估计可靠性，融合语义、词汇和粒度置信度。UMGP-RAG变体使用细粒度命中定位证据并返回非冗余父块保持局部连贯性。

Result: 在问答基准测试中，不确定性感知融合和父块提升方法在保持轻量即插即用管线的前提下提升了生成质量。

Conclusion: 无训练混合粒度检索框架通过不确定性估计有效平衡了检索粒度矛盾，为RAG系统提供了简单高效的改进方案。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) depends critically on the quality and granularity of retrieved evidence. Large retrieval units preserve context but often introduce irrelevant content, which can dilute answer bearing evidence and worsen long context utilization. Fine-grained units are more compact, but they may be difficult to retrieve reliably because short chunks can lack semantic, lexical, or bridging cues needed to match the query. We propose Uncertainty-aware Multi-Granularity RAG (UMG-RAG), a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that treats chunk granularity as query-specific reliability estimation. Instead of training a new retriever or modifying the generator, UMG-RAG uses existing dense and sparse retrievers as complementary experts across multiple chunk granularities. For each query, it converts each expert-granularity score list into an evidence distribution, estimates reliability from distribution entropy, and fuses candidates according to query-specific semantic, lexical, and granularity confidence. We further introduce UMGP-RAG, a parent promotion variant that uses fine-grained hits to locate relevant evidence while returning broader non-redundant parent chunks for local coherence. Experiments on question answering benchmarks show that uncertainty-aware fusion and parent promotion improve generation quality while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play retrieval pipeline.

</details>


### [76] [Scale Buys Interpolation, Structure Buys a Horizon: Certified Predictability for Equivariant World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13092)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文证明等变潜世界模型的可预测地平线是可计算的、多步的，且误差在对称轨道上恒定，并由李雅普诺夫谱分通道确定，提供了唯一的结构性认证；实验表明非等变模型无法获得该认证，且该认证无需校准数据即可审计预训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型的平均误差无法判断单个预测的可信度或持续时间，亟需一种可计算的、结构性的认证方法来量化预测的可靠地平线。

Method: 通过理论分析证明等变潜世界模型的T步展开误差在对称轨道上恒定（定理A），并由预测器的李雅普诺夫谱分层确定地平线T_j(ε)~log(1/ε)/λ_j；同时导出匹配下界，证明近似等变模型的地平线受限，且轨道常数误差唯一定义等变性。

Result: 在40维Lorenz-96系统中，仅等变网络恢复完整李雅普诺夫谱（R²=0.98）；在固定感知预算下，等变认证需c倍预算即可满足，而密集模型无法达到；在TD-MPC2和V-JEPA上，认证准确识别校准、乐观或收缩区域，且校准不随参数规模提升。

Conclusion: 规模只能提供插值，而结构才能提供可校准的认证地平线；等变模型的结构性认证是唯一可部署的审计对象，无需校准数据即可可靠评估预训练世界模型。

Abstract: Scale buys interpolation; structure buys a certified horizon. A world model's average error says nothing about whether a particular prediction can be trusted, or for how long. For equivariant latent world models we give a computable, multi-step certificate of the predictable horizon: $T$-step rollout error is provably constant over each symmetry orbit (Theorem A) and stratified channel-by-channel by the predictor's Lyapunov spectrum, $T_j(ε)\sim\log(1/ε)/λ_j$. The horizon is two-sided -- a matching lower bound makes approximate equivariance provably horizon-limited -- and the certificate is exclusive to structure: orbit-constant error characterizes equivariance, so no non-equivariant model has it at any scale. Empirically, on 40-D Lorenz-96 only a $\mathbb{Z}_N$-equivariant network recovers the full Lyapunov spectrum ($R^2{=}0.98$); dense and recurrent baselines fail.
  Because the spectrum is faithful, the certificate acts, a priori: under a fixed sensing budget a $c\times$-inflated certificate provably needs $c\times$ the budget, and the equivariant certificate meets a budget its inflated dense counterpart cannot -- with zero calibration data. The same read-out, unchanged, audits public pretrained world models training-free: TD-MPC2 checkpoints land on the certificate's own scope taxonomy -- calibrated where strongly expansive (ratio 0.94-1.02), optimistic where weakly expansive, correctly abstaining where contracting -- a map a deployed monitor replicates cell-by-cell, out-of-sample. Across the official 1M-317M multitask ladder, calibration does not improve with parameters. On V-JEPA 2-AC (1B, real robot data) the measured cross-check correctly overrides an over-promising tangent spectrum -- the cross-validated audit, not the raw number, is the deployable object. Scale buys interpolation, not a calibrated horizon.

</details>


### [77] [The Geometry of Phase Transitions in Generative Dynamics via Projection Caustics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13191)
*Ryosuke Sakamoto,Kotaro Sakamoto*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度解释扩散模型中样本发生突变的相变行为，发现该现象源于投影焦散线附近最近点投影的非唯一性，并据此提出临界边界检测器（CBD）作为实用诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型虽采用连续时间逆向动力学，但样本生成过程中常出现突变（如模式锁定、语义坍缩），缺乏几何层面的理论解释。

Method: 将去噪过程视为自由能景观上的梯度下降，通过分析数据支撑集的最近点投影几何，揭示投影焦散线（投影非唯一区域）与突变行为的关联，并设计CBD诊断分数方向不稳定性。

Result: 在玩具模型、标准扩散模型及文本到图像潜在扩散模型中，CBD可定位模式锁定时刻、预测干预敏感窗口，并在几何敏感区域实现针对性控制。

Conclusion: 研究建立了数据几何与扩散生成动力学的联系，为理解和控制生成过程中的突变提供了几何理论框架与实用工具。

Abstract: Continuous-state generative samplers, including diffusion and flow-matching models, evolve through continuous reverse-time dynamics, yet their samples often undergo abrupt qualitative changes: trajectories commit to modes, semantic alternatives collapse, and small perturbations in narrow time windows can produce large downstream effects. This paper develops a geometric account of such phase-transition-like behaviour. We view denoising as gradient descent on a free energy landscape and show that sharp transitions arise near projection caustics, where the nearest-point projection onto the data support ceases to be unique. Motivated by this perspective, we introduce the Critical Boundary Detector (CBD), as practical diagnostics for score-direction instability. Across toy models, standard diffusion models, and latent text-to-image diffusion models, CBD localises mode commitment, predicts intervention-sensitive windows, and supports targeted control in geometrically sensitive regions. Our results connect geometry of data and dynamics of diffusion generation.

</details>


### [78] [Automated reproducibility assessments in the social and behavioral sciences using large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13670)
*Tobias Holtdirk,Pietro Marcolongo,Anna Steinberg Schulten,Felix Henninger,Stefan Rose,Sarah Ball,Bolei Ma,Frauke Kreuter,Markus Weinmann,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 大型语言模型能以较低成本自动化评估社会科学研究的可重复性，效果与人类再分析相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工重分析可重复性耗时费力，难以规模化。

Method: 利用大型语言模型对76篇已发表研究进行再分析，比较其效应量估计与原结果及人类再分析。

Result: 在可计算的69篇中，LLM以±0.05 Cohen's d容差恢复41%原始效应量，定性结论一致率达96%，优于人类再分析的34%和74%。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Reproducibility in the social and behavioral sciences is typically evaluated by independent researchers who reanalyze the original data to assess whether the published findings can be recovered. However, such approaches are resource-intensive and difficult to scale. Here, we show that large language models (LLMs) can automate reproducibility assessments. Using N=76 published studies with predefined claims from the behavioral and social sciences, we compare LLM-generated analysis with the original findings and human reanalysis. For 7 studies, the LLM could not produce a viable effect size estimate. For the remaining studies, our LLM pipeline recovered the original effect sizes in 41% of studies using a +/-0.05 tolerance in Cohen's d. Further, our LLM pipeline reached the same qualitative conclusion as the original study in 96% of cases, where conclusions indicate whether the reanalysis supports the original claim. For comparison, human reanalysts recovered the original effect sizes in 34% of studies and reached the same qualitative conclusion in 74% of cases. Together, these results show that LLMs can serve as a scalable tool for automated reproducibility assessment and provide a foundation for systematic auditing of empirical results in the social and behavioral sciences.

</details>


### [79] [Understanding helpfulness and harmless tension in reward models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13209)
*Eshaan Tanwar,Pepa Atanasova*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 在混合目标奖励模型中，帮助性与无害性目标存在干扰，共享神经元对模型行为产生不成比例的影响，导致对齐张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RLHF中奖励模型帮助性与无害性目标被广泛使用，但两者间的内部冲突机制尚不明确，有必要进行深入研究。

Method: 训练帮助性、无害性及混合目标奖励模型，通过激活基方法识别目标相关神经元，并利用靶向消融探究其功能角色。

Result: 混合目标模型表现差于单目标模型；目标特异性神经元支持自身目标但损害对立目标；共享神经元比例大且对齐张力贡献显著。

Conclusion: 多目标对齐存在内在冲突，未来需要可解耦和可控的对齐方法。

Abstract: Reward models are a key component of reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF), aligning language models toward both helpful and harmless behaviour. However, the internal mechanisms underlying these objectives and their conflicts remain poorly understood. We study alignment tension in reward models trained under helpfulness-only, harmlessness-only, and mixed-objective settings. We find that mixed-objective models often underperform single-objective models, indicating interference between objectives. Using activation-based methods, we identify neurons associated with each objective and study their functional roles via targeted ablations. We find that these neurons causally support their corresponding objectives while often negatively affecting the opposing one. We find that a substantial proportion of neurons are shared between helpfulness and harmlessness, and that these shared neurons exert a disproportionate influence on model behaviour, contributing to alignment tension. Additionally, our results provide insights and mechanistic interpretation into how alignment objectives are represented in reward models and why multi-objective alignment remains challenging, motivating future work on disentangled and controllable alignment methods.

</details>


### [80] [Different Layers, Different Manifolds: Module-Wise Weight-Space Geometry in Transformer Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13276)
*Kirato Yoshihara*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 不同 Transformer 模块对特定的流形几何约束偏好不同，在 GPT-2 预训练中，对注意力层使用 Stiefel 流形、对 MLP 层使用 DGram 流形效果最佳，而反向分配会导致训练不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流形约束通常统一应用于所有权重矩阵，但不同 Transformer 模块可能偏好不同的几何结构，因此需要研究模块特定的几何分配。

Method: 在 GPT-2 预训练中，为注意力层和 MLP 层分别分配 Stiefel 和 DGram 流形约束，并测试不同组合的性能与稳定性。

Result: 注意力层使用 Stiefel、MLP 层使用 DGram 的配置性能最优；反相分配或全 DGram 配置在共享超参数下不稳定，原因是 DGram 约束的注意力权重中奇异值增长导致注意力 logit 放大和 softmax 饱和。

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Weight-space geometry plays a central role in neural network optimization, yet manifold constraints are often applied uniformly across all weight matrices. In this work, we ask whether different transformer modules prefer different manifold geometries. We study Manifold Muon for GPT-2 pretraining and compare layer-wise assignments of Stiefel and DGram constraints across attention and MLP blocks. Our results show a clear asymmetry: constraining attention layers with Stiefel geometry while assigning DGram geometry to MLP layers gives the best performance among the tested configurations, whereas the inverted assignment and all-DGram configuration become unstable under the shared hyperparameter setting. We trace this failure to singular value growth in DGram-constrained attention weights, which can amplify attention logits and induce softmax saturation. These findings suggest that symmetry-aware and geometry-aware optimization for transformers should be module-specific rather than uniform.

</details>


### [81] [Clipping Makes Distributed and Federated Asynchronous SGD Robust to Stragglers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13287)
*Samuel Erickson,Mikael Johansson*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本研究为异步随机梯度下降（ASGD）中梯度裁剪（gradient clipping）的稳定性提供了理论依据，证明裁剪可消除最大延迟对Oracle复杂度的影响，并在次韦布尔噪声模型下首次实现异步优化的高概率收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步训练中慢工作者的延迟更新会对收敛产生负面影响，而经验观察到梯度裁剪能稳定训练过程，但缺乏理论解释。

Method: 采用次韦布尔（sub-Weibull）模型泛化梯度噪声分布（涵盖重尾分布），理论分析梯度裁剪对ASGD收敛性的影响，推导期望收敛与高概率收敛界。

Result: 梯度裁剪消除了最大延迟在Oracle复杂度中的依赖，并首次在异步优化中证明高概率收敛。

Conclusion: 梯度裁剪的理论有效性被严格证明，可缓解异步训练中延迟带来的不稳定问题，为实用优化算法提供理论支撑。

Abstract: In modern machine learning, parallelization of training is an important strategy for increasing scale. Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (ASGD), which maximizes the utilization of available hardware by avoiding waiting for slow workers. However, with constant step sizes, the convergence of ASGD is nonetheless affected negatively by slow workers due to large delays in updates. At the same time, it has been empirically observed in asynchronous training of deep learning models that gradient clipping "stabilizes" training. In this work, we provide a theoretical justification for this behavior, as we show that clipping removes the dependence of the maximum delay in the oracle complexity. We employ a sub-Weibull model of gradient noise which generalizes sub-Gaussian and sub-exponential distributions to more heavy-tailed distributions, motivated by empirical observations in deep learning. We show convergence in expectation, and the first time in asynchronous optimization, convergence with high probability.

</details>


### [82] [Enhanced Low-Density Region Exploration in Classifier-Guided Diffusion Models Through Modified Reverse Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13347)
*Jagriti Singh,Shekhar Verma,Muneendra Ojha*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需额外训练、仅在采样时通过密度感知的引导策略来改善条件扩散模型对长尾稀有样本覆盖的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分类器引导会集中概率质量于高密度类均值，导致对尾部分布稀有样本覆盖不足；现有方法（如低密度搜索分类器）需要额外网络和训练，而加速或蒸馏技术未明确解决长尾覆盖问题。

Method: 在预训练的条件扩散模型和分类器基础上，修改引导反向动力学：一方面通过分类器梯度将轨迹引向低置信区域（探索低概率样本），另一方面在每一步引导采样向预测的真实图像靠近（保持生成样本接近真实数据流形）。

Result: 在ImageNet 64x64分辨率下，ADM模型召回率持续提升，同时FID相当；在256x256分辨率下，展示了不同引导组合的视觉结果，并证明标准ADM分类器引导结合预测真实图像引导能生成高感知质量的样本。

Conclusion: 所提方法在无需额外训练的情况下有效改善了条件扩散模型对长尾样本的覆盖，并保持了生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as state-of-the-art generative models for high-fidelity image synthesis, particularly in their classifier-free guided and classifier-guided forms. However, standard classifier guidance concentrates probability mass around high-density class mean, leading to poor coverage of rare samples in the tails of the class-conditional distributions. Recent work on diffusion-based tail sampling mitigates this by training an additional low-density-seeking classifier with a synthetic-vs-real discriminator, at the cost of additional networks and training. In parallel, a number of samplers and distillation techniques accelerate or refine diffusion sampling, but do not explicitly address long-tail coverage. We propose a purely sampling-time, density-aware extension of classifier-guided conditional diffusion model that targets low-density regions without any additional training. We have applied guidance at noisy images not on predicted noise like most diffusion models. Starting from a pretrained conditional diffusion model and classifier on ImageNet, we modify the guided reverse dynamics by steering trajectories toward low-confidence regions via the modified classifier gradient, and at each time step, we also guide the sampling process toward the predicted real image. 1st guidance helps explore low-probability samples, and 2nd guidance helps to generate samples to be close to the real data manifold. The proposed sampler consistently improves ADM model recall at 64x64 resolution while maintaining a comparable FID, and with a 256x256 ADM model, we showed the results visually with different combinations of both guidance. We also showed that standard ADM classifier guidance, combined with predicted real image guidance, helps generate high perceptual quality samples with a 256x256 ADM model on ImageNet.

</details>


### [83] [Clustering Node Attributed Networks with Graph Neural Networks and Self Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13444)
*Rodrigo de Sapienza Luna,Daniel Ratton Figueiredo*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 提出一种基于图神经网络的自学习框架，通过多轮迭代聚类优化图结构，在平衡簇规模的数据集上达到先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图聚类算法未能有效结合节点属性与网络结构信息，且传统GNN单轮训练性能受限。

Method: 构建无监督自学习框架，每轮用GNN生成节点表示后聚类，聚类结果影响下一轮图结构，同时保留原始图上下文信息。

Result: 在合成数据上同时利用边和属性优于单一方法；多轮学习始终优于单轮；真实数据平衡簇场景与先进方法相当。

Conclusion: 提出多轮自学习框架能动态优化图表示，在平衡簇场景下有效提取节点属性与结构信息。

Abstract: Graph clustering - partitioning the node set of a graph into disjoint subsets that reflect some latent information - is a fundamental problem as it finds applications in a myriad of different scenarios. While this classic problem has been tackled for decades by different communities, a recent variation of the problem driven by real data considers the scenario where nodes have attributes that are also informative. This has triggered novel methods that simultaneously leverage network information (edges) and node information (attributed) in the design of novel clustering algorithms. This work proposes a novel framework that builds on prior works that have applied graph neural networks (GNN) to graph clustering. The proposed framework operates in rounds of self learning in a fully unsupervised setting. In each round, a GNN generates representations for nodes that are used to cluster the nodes. This clustering influences the graph used to generate the node representation in the next round. Moreover, a context graph built in each round using the original graph is used to generate the node representations. Empirical results show that the proposed methodology extracts information from both network edges and node attributes in synthetic data, outperforming algorithms focused solely on the network or attributes when neither are very informative. Multiple rounds of learning also improve the performance and always outperforms a long single round of training (i.e., classic GNN graph clustering). When considering real datasets, empirical results indicate that the proposed methodology is competitive to state-of-the-art methods when cluster sizes are balanced.

</details>


### [84] [The Stable Recovery Manifold: Geometric Principles Governing Recoverability in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13637)
*Ayushman Trivedi,Bhavika Melwani*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 遗忘知识在表征重组后仍可紧凑解码，灾难性遗忘主要是可访问性与流形对齐问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Accessibility Collapse框架，探究持续学习中可恢复性的几何结构，验证遗忘是信息破坏还是访问问题。

Method: 在Split CIFAR-100上用ResNet-18顺序训练，引入恢复子空间维度k_t度量，分析可恢复性、表征漂移与恢复复杂度。

Result: 恢复子空间维度稳定（均值k_t=8.0），主角度漂移强预测可恢复性（r=-0.862），几何模型解释82.2%方差。

Conclusion: 支持稳定恢复流形假说：遗忘知识仍可紧凑解码，灾难性遗忘本质是流形对齐与可访问性问题。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often viewed as the destruction of previously learned knowledge during sequential learning. Building on the Accessibility Collapse framework, we investigate the geometric structure of recoverability in continual learning. Using Split CIFAR-100 and a sequentially trained ResNet-18, we analyze recoverability, representational drift, and recovery complexity across ten tasks. We introduce Recovery Subspace Dimensionality (k_t), a measure of the minimum number of singular directions required to preserve 90 percent of full probe performance. Contrary to our Recoverability Diffusion hypothesis, recovery dimensionality remains stable throughout training (mean k_t = 8.0) despite substantial representational drift. Principal-angle drift strongly predicts recoverability (r = -0.862), and a simple geometric model explains 82.2 percent of recoverability variance. These findings support the Stable Recovery Manifold hypothesis, suggesting that forgotten knowledge remains compactly decodable despite representational reorganization. The results indicate that catastrophic forgetting is primarily an accessibility and manifold-alignment problem rather than information destruction.

</details>


### [85] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: Training

TL;DR: 本文分析了在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的参数更新特性，发现其具有稀疏性和特定几何结构，并通过子网训练和优化器消融实验验证了这些特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD结合了在线策略学生轨迹和密集教师监督，但其对模型参数的具体影响尚不清楚。

Method: 对多种语言和视觉-语言模型进行OPD更新分析，包括稀疏性（更新幅度、坐标稀疏性、层分布、FFN主导性）和几何特性（数值满秩、谱集中、主奇异子空间外、零权重坐标上的变化）。

Result: OPD更新稀疏且集中在FFN层，训练子网可恢复接近完整OPD性能；SGD优化器不如AdamW；更新数值满秩但谱集中，主要落在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未使OPD成为普通密集参数重写，而是保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe as it combines two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision, yet how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD}-style updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers and are usually FFN-heavy. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork recovers nearly the same performance as full \textsc{OPD}. However, the sparsity-inducing SGD optimizer underperforms AdamW in our optimizer ablation, likely because dense teacher supervision preserves heterogeneous coordinate-wise gradient scales where AdamW's adaptive scaling remains useful. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


<div id='Inference'></div>

# Inference [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ToolSense诊断框架，发现参数化工具检索模型在模糊查询下性能大幅下降，且存在知识与检索的分离问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准使用完整查询和约束解码，无法真实反映模型对工具的理解，且存在检索与知识分离的潜在问题。

Method: 构建ToolSense框架，自动生成三类基准：多层级模糊真实检索基准(RRB)、多项选择探查基准和问答探查基准。

Result: 在ToolBench上，多个参数化模型在RRB上性能下降50-64个百分点，低于嵌入基线；部分模型在事实探查中接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 参数化工具检索存在知识与检索的分离，现有基准掩盖了模型的实际理解能力缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [87] [CAPED: Context-Aware Privacy Exposure Defense for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12666)
*Siyu Shen,Fenghao Xu,Wenrui Diao,Kehuan Zhang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出CAPED框架，通过任务驱动的选择性暴露机制减少移动GUI代理中的偶然视觉隐私泄露，在保护隐私的同时维持任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截图式移动GUI代理在正常操作中会暴露大量无关敏感信息（如联系人、消息等），现有防御手段（文本匿名化或通用隐私掩码）难以平衡隐私保护与任务完成需求。

Method: CAPED作为手机端保护层，在截图上传前提取任务需求、利用屏幕上下文作为隐私先验、解析UI元素，仅暴露当前任务所需内容，掩蔽偶然私有内容。

Result: 在28项种子隐私评估中，CAPED将成功条件加权种子泄露从0.766降至0.268，同时保持高任务效用；AndroidWorld评估显示有原型级效用损失，但验证了选择性暴露的可行性。

Conclusion: 截图上传应视为设备-云边界的明确决策，需由任务驱动的选择性暴露而非全有或全无的屏幕共享来管理。

Abstract: Screenshot-based mobile GUI agents can operate ordinary smartphone apps through the same visual interface as a human user, but this capability also turns every screen observation into a privacy boundary. During normal task execution, screenshots may expose contacts, messages, photos, files, recommendations, health cues, and other sensitive context that is unrelated to the user's request. We call this problem incidental visual privacy exposure. It is difficult to address with existing defenses: text anonymization misses many visual and inferential cues, while generic privacy masking can remove the evidence and controls that a GUI agent needs to complete the task.
  This paper presents CAPED, a context-aware pre-upload exposure control layer for mobile GUI agents. CAPED is designed as a phone-side protection layer: before screenshots are released to a remote multimodal agent, it extracts task requirements, uses screen context as a privacy prior, parses visible UI elements, and selectively exposes only content needed for the current task while masking incidental private content. We evaluate CAPED on AndroidWorld for broad task utility and with a controlled 28-task seeded privacy evaluation used as a measurement instrument for trajectory-level incidental leakage. In this seeded evaluation, Full CAPED reduces success-conditioned weighted seeded leakage from 0.766 under raw screenshots to 0.268 while preserving high task utility. A broader AndroidWorld run shows a remaining prototype-level utility cost, but the results support the central claim that screenshot upload should be treated as an explicit device--cloud boundary decision, governed by task-driven selective exposure rather than all-or-nothing screen sharing.

</details>


### [88] [Arbor: Tree Search as a Cognition Layer for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12563)
*Neha Prakriya,Chaojun Hou,Zheng Gong,Huasha Zhao,Xi Zhao,Mou Li,Zhenyu Gu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: Arbor是一个多智能体框架，通过结构化树搜索作为认知层，实现大型有状态动作空间中的自主优化，在LLM推理优化中取得高达193%的帕累托改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主优化系统局限于孤立目标和无状态评估，无法应对需要跨全栈协调的复杂优化任务，且单智能体方法易陷入不可恢复的崩溃。

Method: 维护一个显式搜索树作为多智能体共享工作记忆，由Orchestrator（协调者）和Critic（批评者）智能体组成制衡架构，将能力分解为硬技能（领域知识）和软技能（协调协议）。

Result: 在LLM推理优化中实现最高193%的吞吐量-延迟帕累托改进，单智能体仅提升33%且数小时内崩溃；跨多代硬件泛化，运行间方差小于2个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化树搜索与多智能体制衡架构相结合，能在复杂全栈优化场景中实现硬件无关、可复现的自主优化，显著超越传统单智能体方法。

Abstract: Arbor is a multi-agent framework that introduces structured tree search as a cognition layer for autonomous agents operating in large, stateful action spaces. Prior autonomous optimization systems operate on isolated targets with stateless evaluation. Arbor instead maintains an explicit search tree of scored hypotheses that serves as the shared working memory across agents, evolving with every measurement, treating failures as diagnostic signal that reshapes subsequent exploration, and expanding as prior successes shift the bottleneck distribution.
  We validate Arbor on full-stack LLM inference optimization, a domain where achieving peak performance has historically required coordinated effort from engineering teams across the application, framework, compiler, kernel, and hardware stack. Arbor pairs an Orchestrator agent, which drives optimization by delegating to Domain Specialists across the inference stack, with a Critic agent that safeguards stability through root-cause analysis, introspection, and measurement validation -- a checks-and-balances architecture where neither agent can unilaterally drive the system. Agent capabilities are decomposed into hard skills (domain expertise) and soft skills (coordination protocols that determine how contributions compose), enabling fully autonomous multi-day campaigns. Arbor achieves up to 193% inference throughput-latency Pareto improvement over vendor-optimized baselines, while a single agent without the harness plateaus at +33% throughput improvement and crashes irrecoverably within hours. Arbor generalizes to multiple generations of hardware platform, and run-to-run variance is within 2 percentage points demonstrating that the method is hardware-agnostic and reproducible.

</details>


### [89] [Strategic Decision Support for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12587)
*Shayan Kiyani,Sima Noorani,George Pappas,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向AI代理的战略决策支持框架，通过优化问题最小化支持使用并控制反事实遗漏支持错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策支持中人类使用模型，现代代理系统角色反转，AI代理成为核心行动者，需要解决其可靠性问题和与人类目标对齐的挑战。

Method: 将决策支持建模为约束优化问题，推导出最优阈值策略，开发在线算法自适应调整阈值并利用随机探索控制错误，引入即时校准方法减少不必要支持。

Result: 在信息收集、人机协作和工具使用等多种场景中，方法能可靠控制目标错误率并显著减少支持调用次数。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于现代AI代理系统，有效平衡了支持成本与决策可靠性。

Abstract: Traditionally, decision support studies how humans use machine learning models to make better decisions.
